# META
~~~ini
description=Quantity folding uses settled unit identities and carries them through contextual evaluation
type=file
libraries=standard
~~~
# SOURCE
~~~sysml
package QuantityEvaluation {
    private import SI::*;
    private alias massUnit for SI::kilogram;

    attribute base = 2 [kg];
    attribute sum = base + 3 [kilogram];
    constraint spelling { 2 [kg] == 2 [kilogram] }
    constraint aliasSpelling { base == 2 [massUnit] }
    constraint addition { sum == 5 [kilogram] }
    constraint distinctUnits { 2 [kg] == 2 [g] }

    attribute def Budget {
        attribute load : ISQ::MassValue;
        constraint within { load <= base }
    }
    attribute passing : Budget { :>> load = 1 [kilogram]; }
    attribute failing : Budget { :>> load = 3 [kilogram]; }

    package Shadow {
        part def kg;
        attribute invalid = 2 [kg];
        constraint shadowed { 2 [kg] == 2 [SI::kg] }
    }

    attribute interval = 20 ['°C_abs'];
    attribute acceleration = 1 [m / s^2];
    constraint scaleComparison { interval == 20 ['°C_abs'] }
    constraint compoundComparison { acceleration == 1 [m / s^2] }
}
~~~
